# Agents · S · part 2

965 entries, s55-builder to Salesforce Flow Development. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **s55-builder**: Sonnet 5.5 builder on high, the default worker for clearly defined work inside an approved plan: a script, a feature in one area, tests, UI that follows an existing pattern, a fix coming back from review. The router confirms or moves each builder brief between s55-builder, s55-builder-deep and o55-builder-precise. Edits files and runs tests by itself. ([stas4000/claude-subagent-router](https://github.com/stas4000/claude-subagent-router/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/s55-builder.md) · ★10)
- **s55-builder-deep**: Sonnet 5.5 builder on extra high, for well specified work that is bigger than one area: several files in one subsystem, a new module or small package with a clear contract, a full-stack feature inside one app. Not for shared live state, money, auth, sends or a repeat after failure: those go to o55-builder-precise. The router confirms or changes the pick. Edits files and runs tests by itself. ([stas4000/claude-subagent-router](https://github.com/stas4000/claude-subagent-router/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/s55-builder-deep.md) · ★10)
- **s55-scout**: Sonnet 5.5 on low, read-only code and file search. Use to locate code, callers, config, logs or docs when the lead knows what it needs but not where it lives, instead of reading many files itself. Returns paths with line ranges and one line each, never file dumps. Never edits. ([stas4000/claude-subagent-router](https://github.com/stas4000/claude-subagent-router/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/s55-scout.md) · ★10)
- **s55-verifier**: Sonnet 5.5 verifier on medium, the default verifier. Use after every builder pass: checks the brief's acceptance clauses by running real checks, probes edge cases, may add tests but never changes implementation code (a hook refuses it). Reports what passed, what failed and what remains uncertain; failures go back to the builder. ([stas4000/claude-subagent-router](https://github.com/stas4000/claude-subagent-router/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/s55-verifier.md) · ★10)
- **s6-legal**: Sanibha playbook S6 (legal and compliance) agent. Company accounts and contracts, privacy policy and labels, sector rules, subscription and ad law, name clearance, submission compliance, incident plan. Prepares lawyer/CA questions; gives no legal advice. Use when asked to run, audit or prepare this stage for an app. ([Sanibha-Pvt-Ltd/company-brain](https://github.com/Sanibha-Pvt-Ltd/company-brain/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/s6-legal.md))
- **s7-submission**: Sanibha playbook S7 (App Store submission and ASO) agent. Listing assets, submission package, reviewer notes, guideline self-review, rejection playbook, update cadence. Use when asked to run, audit or prepare this stage for an app. ([Sanibha-Pvt-Ltd/company-brain](https://github.com/Sanibha-Pvt-Ltd/company-brain/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/s7-submission.md))
- **s8-marketing**: Sanibha playbook S8 (marketing and growth) agent. Channel and budget rules, Meta and Apple Search Ads lead times, creative pipeline, ad-claim review, organic and reputation. Use when asked to run, audit or prepare this stage for an app. ([Sanibha-Pvt-Ltd/company-brain](https://github.com/Sanibha-Pvt-Ltd/company-brain/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/s8-marketing.md))
- **s9-launch**: Sanibha playbook S9 (launch) agent. Dated T-30 to T+30 launch timeline, no-go list, launch-day runbook, kill/scale checkpoints. Use when asked to run, audit or prepare this stage for an app. ([Sanibha-Pvt-Ltd/company-brain](https://github.com/Sanibha-Pvt-Ltd/company-brain/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/s9-launch.md))

- **sa**: System Analyst — bridge to domain experts. Discovery, user stories, feature specs with acceptance criteria (Given/When/Then). Used at the design phase before code. Call `5 D T`. ([BilBBBOBaggins/emcee](https://github.com/BilBBBOBaggins/emcee/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sa.md) · ★3)
- **sa**: 架構師（SA）。兩種模式的架構判斷——對一段改動做審查（模組邊界、接縫位置、不變式歸屬、單一事實來源、失敗模式、多餘的抽象與過渡期程式的去留，與專案詞彙表的相容性），或不看 diff 掃整個 codebase 找值得加深的模組。當使用者要求架構審查、質疑某個設計是否放對位置、要評估一次改動有沒有把耦合留在錯的地方，或想知道這個 codebase 接下來該改善哪裡時使用。它只提出判斷與取捨，不改任何檔案；行級缺陷與清理交給 code review。 ([gunter1020/devteam](https://github.com/gunter1020/devteam/blob/HEAD/agents/sa.md) · ★1)
- **sa**: 售前解决方案工程师角色总入口（Role Meta-Agent）— 你只需要一句自然语言告诉我你要做什么， 我会拆解成 3-8 步执行计划，展示给你确认后自动依次调用具体命令。 专注端到端 7 模块流水线（M1 输入接入 → M2 客户调研 → M3 方案拆解 → M4 总体方案架构 → M5 POC/实施/RFP → M6 报价宣讲 → M7 归档）。 服务对外客户售前项目场景，与服务内部 PRD 场景的 @pm 互补。 ([yf-hash/WorkAgent](https://github.com/yf-hash/WorkAgent/blob/HEAD/agents/sa.md) · ★1)
- **sa**: System Architect — the autonomous orchestrator. Invoke to drive epic execution through Plan, Dispatch, Audit, Review, Smoke, Validate, Close-prep, and Close-finalize phases. Spawns all other agents as subagents. Does not write implementation code. ([jasgr-software/agentstack](https://github.com/jasgr-software/agentstack/blob/HEAD/agents/sa.md))
- **sa**: 系統分析師 (System Analyst)。專責釐清需求、撰寫 User Story、拆解功能模組， 並產出 spec / plan 交給 developer agent 執行。 適用情境： - 使用者描述模糊需求（「幫我做一個查股票的功能」） - 需要拆解成多個子任務再分派 - 需要釐清邊界條件、資料來源、API 設計 - 需要產出 spec-kit（需求規格 + plan）再交給 developer 禁止：不得自行撰寫業務程式碼，需求分析完畢後一律回報 orchestrator。 ([Josephmtsai/fastApiStock](https://github.com/Josephmtsai/fastApiStock/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sa.md))
- **sa**: 솔루션 아키텍트. Phase 0(Discovery)에서 인프라/네트워크 아키텍처 설계, 기술 선택 및 대안 비교, 기술 검토서 작성을 수행한다. Gate 2의 architect(TA)가 '어떻게 만들지'를 명세하는 반면, SA는 '무엇을/왜 선택하는지'를 결정한다. ([julyinsung/Vulcan-Anvil](https://github.com/julyinsung/Vulcan-Anvil/blob/HEAD/templates/.claude/agents/sa.md))
